Q293. Between \(214\) and \(374\), how many integers are divisible by \(18\)?
A. \(11\)
B. \(9\)
C. \(10\)
D. \(8\)
Answer: B

Explanation:
Step 1: Find the first valid multiple

Therefore, the first multiple of 18 greater than 214 is 216.

\[
18\times 12=216
\]

Step 2: Find the last valid multiple

Therefore, 360 is the greatest multiple of 18 not exceeding 374.

\[
374=18\times 20+14
\]

\[
374-14=360
\]

Step 3: Count the valid multiples

The multiples form an arithmetic progression with common difference 18.

\[
\frac{360-216}{18}+1=8+1=9
\]

Answer Verification
The total number of multiples is 9.
Hence, there are 9 multiples of 18 in the given range.

Final Answer
Therefore, the required answer is \(9\). Hence, the correct answer is Option (B).

Shortcut

Count multiples using quotient floors on the range limits.

\[
\left\lfloor\frac{374}{18}\right\rfloor-\left\lfloor\frac{213}{18}\right\rfloor
\]

\[
20-11=9
\]

Fast result: \(9\). Therefore, the correct answer is Option (B).

Hindi:
\(214\) और \(374\) के बीच कितने पूर्णांक \(18\) से विभाज्य हैं?
चरण 1: पहला मान्य गुणज ज्ञात करें

अतः 214 से बड़ा 18 का पहला गुणज 216 है।

\[
18\times 12=216
\]

चरण 2: आखिरी वैध गुणज ज्ञात करें

अतः 360, 374 से अधिक न होने वाला 18 का सबसे बड़ा गुणज है।

\[
374=18\times 20+14
\]

\[
374-14=360
\]

चरण 3: मान्य गुणजों की गिनती करें

गुणज 18 के सामान्य अंतर वाली समांतर श्रेणी बनाते हैं।

\[
\frac{360-216}{18}+1=8+1=9
\]

उत्तर सत्यापन
गुणजों की कुल संख्या 9 है।
अतः दी गई सीमा में 18 के 9 गुणज हैं।

अंतिम उत्तर
अतः सही उत्तर विकल्प (B) है। आवश्यक मान \(9\) है।

शॉर्टकट

सीमा की सीमाओं पर भागफल-मूल्य से गुणज गिनें।

\[
\left\lfloor\frac{374}{18}\right\rfloor-\left\lfloor\frac{213}{18}\right\rfloor
\]

\[
20-11=9
\]

शीघ्र परिणाम: \(9\)। अतः सही उत्तर विकल्प (B) है।

Punjabi:
\(214\) ਅਤੇ \(374\) ਦੇ ਵਿਚਕਾਰ ਕਿੰਨੇ ਪੂਰਨ ਅੰਕ \(18\) ਨਾਲ ਭਾਗ ਜਾਂਦੇ ਹਨ?
ਕਦਮ 1: ਪਹਿਲਾ ਵੈਧ ਗੁਣਜ ਕੱਢੋ

ਇਸ ਲਈ 214 ਤੋਂ ਵੱਡਾ 18 ਦਾ ਪਹਿਲਾ ਗੁਣਜ 216 ਹੈ।

\[
18\times 12=216
\]

ਕਦਮ 2: ਆਖਰੀ ਵੈਧ ਗੁਣਜ ਕੱਢੋ

ਇਸ ਲਈ 360, 374 ਤੋਂ ਵੱਧ ਨਾ ਹੋਣ ਵਾਲਾ 18 ਦਾ ਸਭ ਤੋਂ ਵੱਡਾ ਗੁਣਜ ਹੈ।

\[
374=18\times 20+14
\]

\[
374-14=360
\]

ਕਦਮ 3: ਵੈਧ ਗੁਣਜਾਂ ਦੀ ਗਿਣਤੀ ਕਰੋ

ਗੁਣਜ 18 ਦੇ ਸਾਂਝੇ ਅੰਤਰ ਵਾਲੀ ਸਮਾਂਤਰ ਸ਼੍ਰੇਣੀ ਬਣਾਉਂਦੇ ਹਨ।

\[
\frac{360-216}{18}+1=8+1=9
\]

ਉੱਤਰ ਪੁਸ਼ਟੀ
ਗੁਣਜਾਂ ਦੀ ਕੁੱਲ ਗਿਣਤੀ 9 ਹੈ।
ਇਸ ਲਈ ਦਿੱਤੀ ਸੀਮਾ ਵਿੱਚ 18 ਦੇ 9 ਗੁਣਜ ਹਨ।

ਅੰਤਿਮ ਉੱਤਰ
ਇਸ ਲਈ ਸਹੀ ਉੱਤਰ ਵਿਕਲਪ (B) ਹੈ। ਲੋੜੀਂਦਾ ਮੁੱਲ \(9\) ਹੈ।

ਛੋਟਾ ਤਰੀਕਾ

ਸੀਮਾ ਦੀਆਂ ਹੱਦਾਂ ਤੇ ਭਾਗਫਲ-ਮੂਲਾਂ ਨਾਲ ਗੁਣਜ ਗਿਣੋ।

\[
\left\lfloor\frac{374}{18}\right\rfloor-\left\lfloor\frac{213}{18}\right\rfloor
\]

\[
20-11=9
\]

ਛੇਤੀ ਨਤੀਜਾ: \(9\)। ਇਸ ਲਈ ਸਹੀ ਉੱਤਰ ਵਿਕਲਪ (B) ਹੈ।

Family: ns_cyclic_pattern

---
Q294. Reduce the power \(7^{111}\) by its remainder pattern. What is the remainder on division by \(19\)?
A. \(2\)
B. \(0\)
C. \(3\)
D. \(1\)
Answer: D

Explanation:
Step 1: Reduce the base modulo the divisor

\[
7\equiv 7\pmod{19}
\]

Step 2: Write the remainder cycle

Cycle length = 3.

\[
7\rightarrow 11\rightarrow 1
\]

Step 3: Locate the exponent position in the cycle

\[
(111-1)\bmod 3+1=3
\]

Step 4: Read the required remainder

Hence, remainder = 1.

\[
7^{111}\equiv 1\pmod{19}
\]

Answer Verification
The 3rd term of the cycle is 1.

Final Answer
Therefore, the required answer is \(1\). Hence, the correct answer is Option (D).

Shortcut

Reduce the base once, then jump to the exponent position in the cycle.

\[
p=(111-1)\bmod 3+1=3
\]

\[
7^{111}\equiv 1\pmod{19}
\]

Fast result: \(1\). Therefore, the correct answer is Option (D).

Hindi:
\(7^{111}\) को उसके शेषफल पैटर्न से घटाएं। \(19\) से भाग देने पर शेषफल क्या होगा?
चरण 1: आधार को भाजक के अनुसार घटाएं

\[
7\equiv 7\pmod{19}
\]

चरण 2: शेषफल चक्र लिखें

चक्र की लंबाई = 3।

\[
7\rightarrow 11\rightarrow 1
\]

चरण 3: चक्र में घात की स्थिति ज्ञात करें

\[
(111-1)\bmod 3+1=3
\]

चरण 4: आवश्यक शेषफल पढ़ें

अतः शेषफल = 1।

\[
7^{111}\equiv 1\pmod{19}
\]

उत्तर सत्यापन
चक्र का 3वाँ पद 1 है।

अंतिम उत्तर
अतः सही उत्तर विकल्प (D) है। आवश्यक मान \(1\) है।

शॉर्टकट

आधार एक बार घटाकर चक्र में घात की स्थिति पर जाएं।

\[
p=(111-1)\bmod 3+1=3
\]

\[
7^{111}\equiv 1\pmod{19}
\]

शीघ्र परिणाम: \(1\)। अतः सही उत्तर विकल्प (D) है।

Punjabi:
\(7^{111}\) ਨੂੰ ਉਸ ਦੇ ਬਾਕੀ ਪੈਟਰਨ ਨਾਲ ਘਟਾਓ। \(19\) ਨਾਲ ਭਾਗ ਦੇਣ ਤੇ ਬਾਕੀ ਕੀ ਹੋਵੇਗਾ?
ਕਦਮ 1: ਆਧਾਰ ਨੂੰ ਭਾਜਕ ਮੁਤਾਬਕ ਘਟਾਓ

\[
7\equiv 7\pmod{19}
\]

ਕਦਮ 2: ਬਾਕੀ ਚੱਕਰ ਲਿਖੋ

ਚੱਕਰ ਦੀ ਲੰਬਾਈ = 3।

\[
7\rightarrow 11\rightarrow 1
\]

ਕਦਮ 3: ਚੱਕਰ ਵਿੱਚ ਘਾਤ ਦੀ ਸਥਿਤੀ ਕੱਢੋ

\[
(111-1)\bmod 3+1=3
\]

ਕਦਮ 4: ਲੋੜੀਂਦਾ ਬਾਕੀ ਪੜ੍ਹੋ

ਇਸ ਲਈ ਬਾਕੀ = 1।

\[
7^{111}\equiv 1\pmod{19}
\]

ਉੱਤਰ ਪੁਸ਼ਟੀ
ਚੱਕਰ ਦਾ 3ਵਾਂ ਪਦ 1 ਹੈ।

ਅੰਤਿਮ ਉੱਤਰ
ਇਸ ਲਈ ਸਹੀ ਉੱਤਰ ਵਿਕਲਪ (D) ਹੈ। ਲੋੜੀਂਦਾ ਮੁੱਲ \(1\) ਹੈ।

ਛੋਟਾ ਤਰੀਕਾ

ਆਧਾਰ ਇੱਕ ਵਾਰ ਘਟਾ ਕੇ ਚੱਕਰ ਵਿੱਚ ਘਾਤ ਦੀ ਸਥਿਤੀ ਤੇ ਜਾਓ।

\[
p=(111-1)\bmod 3+1=3
\]

\[
7^{111}\equiv 1\pmod{19}
\]

ਛੇਤੀ ਨਤੀਜਾ: \(1\)। ਇਸ ਲਈ ਸਹੀ ਉੱਤਰ ਵਿਕਲਪ (D) ਹੈ।

Q295. What is the greatest number less than \(2766\) that is divisible by both \(30\) and \(36\)?
A. \(2430\)
B. \(2700\)
C. \(2970\)
D. \(3240\)
Answer: B

Explanation:
Step 1: Combine the number conditions first

First combine the divisibility facts. A number divisible by each given divisor must be a multiple of their LCM.

\[
m=180
\]

Step 2: Choose the number that fits the given limit

Now use the nearby multiples and choose the first or last one according to the question.

\[
q=\left\lfloor\frac{2765}{180}\right\rfloor
\]

\[
N=180\times q=2700
\]

Answer Verification
The selected number is 2700, and it satisfies the required divisibility condition.
\[
N=2700
\]

Final Answer
Therefore, the required answer is \(2700\). Hence, the correct answer is Option (B).

Shortcut

Take the LCM once, then jump directly to the nearest multiple asked in the question.

\[
Z=2700
\]

Fast result: \(2700\). Therefore, the correct answer is Option (B).

Hindi:

Q296. The number \(297\) has to be reduced to a multiple of \(12\). What is the minimum subtraction?
A. \(8\)
B. \(11\)
C. \(9\)
D. \(10\)
Answer: C

Explanation:
Step 1: Combine the number conditions first

First combine the divisibility facts. A number divisible by each given divisor must be a multiple of their LCM.

Step 2: Choose the number that fits the given limit

Now use the nearby multiples and choose the first or last one according to the question.

\[
297=12\times 24+9
\]

\[
x=9
\]

Answer Verification
The selected number is 9, and it satisfies the required divisibility condition.
\[
N=9
\]

Final Answer
Therefore, the required answer is \(9\). Hence, the correct answer is Option (C).

Shortcut

Take the LCM once, then jump directly to the nearest multiple asked in the question.

\[
Z=9
\]

Fast result: \(9\). Therefore, the correct answer is Option (C).

\[

\]

The selected number is 6, and it satisfies the required divisibility condition.

Q298. How many integers between \(181\) and \(559\) leave remainder \(1\) when divided by \(36\)?
A. \(13\)
B. \(11\)
C. \(12\)
D. \(10\)
Answer: B

Explanation:
Step 1: Combine the number conditions first

First combine the divisibility facts. A number divisible by each given divisor must be a multiple of their LCM.

Step 2: Choose the number that fits the given limit

Now use the nearby multiples and choose the first or last one according to the question.

\[
N\equiv 1\pmod{36}
\]

\[
c=11
\]

Answer Verification
The selected number is 11, and it satisfies the required divisibility condition.
\[
N=11
\]

Final Answer
Therefore, the required answer is \(11\). Hence, the correct answer is Option (B).

Shortcut

Take the LCM once, then jump directly to the nearest multiple asked in the question.

\[
Z=11
\]

Fast result: \(11\). Therefore, the correct answer is Option (B).

Q299. For powers of \(2\) modulo \(7\), the remainder is \(2\). Which exponent between \(34\) and \(40\) gives this remainder?
A. \(33\)
B. \(36\)
C. \(34\)
D. \(35\)
Answer: C

Explanation:
Step 1: Translate every clue into a number condition

Reconstruction questions should not be guessed; each clue must become a checkable relation.

\[
2^{e}\equiv 2\pmod{7}
\]

Step 2: Carry the result through the next condition

Check the number against the next fact also, so it is not chosen from only the first clue.

\[
e=34
\]

Answer Verification
The reconstructed value is 34.
\[
N=34
\]

Final Answer
Therefore, the required answer is \(34\). Hence, the correct answer is Option (C).

Shortcut

Start with the clue that gives the smallest search, then test the remaining condition.

\[
Z=34
\]

Fast result: \(34\). Therefore, the correct answer is Option (C).

Hindi:
\(2\) की घातों को \(7\) से भाग देने पर शेष \(2\) मिलता है। \(34\) और \(40\) के बीच कौन-सा घातांक यह शेष देता है?

Q300. A number leaves remainder \(11\) when divided by both \(6\) and \(16\). If it is the \(9\)-th such number after zero, what is the number?
A. \(421\)
B. \(443\)
C. \(465\)
D. \(487\)
Answer: B

Reconstruction questions should not be guessed; each clue must become a checkable relation.

\[
N=48\times 9+11
\]

Step 2: Carry the result through the next condition

Check the number against the next fact also, so it is not chosen from only the first clue.

\[
N=443
\]

Answer Verification
The reconstructed value is 443.
\[
N=443
\]

Final Answer
Therefore, the required answer is \(443\). Hence, the correct answer is Option (B).

Shortcut

Start with the clue that gives the smallest search, then test the remaining condition.

\[
Z=443
